+    private void UnsubscribeSceneEvents()

+    private void OnDisable() => StopVehicleEngine();
+
+    private void LateUpdate()
+    {
+        if (engineProfile == null)
+            return;
+        if (engineOwner == null || !engineOwner.isActiveAndEnabled)
+        {
+            StopVehicleEngine();
+            return;
+        }
+
+        // Slow-motion changes the traversal pace, never the soundtrack clock.
+        float delta = Mathf.Max(0f, Time.unscaledDeltaTime);
+        float response = TeamVehicleAudioRules.IsFinite(engineProfile.pitchResponse)
+            ? Mathf.Max(0f, engineProfile.pitchResponse) : 7f;
+        engineBlendIntensity = Mathf.MoveTowards(engineBlendIntensity, engineIntensity, response * delta);
+        foreach (AudioSource source in engineSources)
+            source.pitch = Mathf.MoveTowards(source.pitch, engineTargetPitch, response * delta);
+        if (!engineEnding)
+            engineEnvelope = Mathf.MoveTowards(engineEnvelope, 1f, delta / 0.025f);
+        ApplyEngineVolume();
+    }

+    public static void BeginVehicleEngine(MonoBehaviour owner, TeamId team, int gear, float completedCells = 0f)
+    {
+        if (instance == null || owner == null || !owner.isActiveAndEnabled)
+            return;
+        instance.StartVehicleEngine(owner, team, completedCells);
+    }
+
+    public static void UpdateVehicleEngine(MonoBehaviour owner, float completedCells)
+    {
+        if (instance == null || instance.engineOwner != owner || instance.engineProfile == null)
+            return;
+        if (owner == null || !owner.isActiveAndEnabled)
+        {
+            instance.StopVehicleEngine();
+            return;
+        }
+        instance.engineIntensity = TeamVehicleAudioRules.EvaluateIntensity(
+            completedCells, instance.engineProfile.cellsToMax);
+        instance.engineTargetPitch = TeamVehicleAudioRules.GetEnginePitch(instance.engineProfile, completedCells);
+    }
+
+    public static void EndVehicleEngine(MonoBehaviour owner, bool immediate = false)
+    {
+        if (instance == null || instance.engineOwner != owner || instance.engineProfile == null)
+            return;
+        if (immediate || !Application.isPlaying || owner == null || !owner.isActiveAndEnabled)
+        {
+            instance.StopVehicleEngine();
+            return;
+        }
+        if (instance.engineEnding)
+            return;
+        if (instance.engineCue != null)
+        {
+            instance.StopCoroutine(instance.engineCue);
+            instance.engineCue = null;
+        }
+        instance.engineEnding = true;
+        instance.engineFade = instance.StartCoroutine(instance.FadeOutVehicleEngine(instance.engineVersion));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Call only after the race rule has successfully committed an actual change.</summary>
+    public static void PlayTeamGearShift(TeamId team, int previousGear, int targetGear)
+    {
+        if (instance == null || !Application.isPlaying || previousGear == targetGear)
+            return;
+        TeamVehicleAudioProfile profile = TeamVehicleAudioRules.GetProfile(team);
+        string path = TeamVehicleAudioRules.GetShiftResourcePath(profile, previousGear, targetGear);
+        AudioClip clip = instance.LoadClip(path);
+        if (clip == null)
+            clip = instance.LoadClip(AudioRuntimeRules.GetSfxPath(AudioEventNames.GearShift));
+        if (clip == null || !instance.throttle.ShouldPlay(path ?? AudioEventNames.GearShift, Time.unscaledTime, 0.04f))
+            return;
+        instance.sfxSource.pitch = 1f;
+        instance.sfxSource.PlayOneShot(clip, profile != null ? SafeGain(profile.shiftGain) : 0.65f);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Short rev for a committed non-animated bonus, never for a visual teleport.</summary>
+    public static void PlayTeamAccelerationCue(MonoBehaviour owner, TeamId team, int gear, int cells)
+    {
+        if (cells <= 0 || instance == null || !Application.isPlaying || owner == null || !owner.isActiveAndEnabled)
+            return;
+        BeginVehicleEngine(owner, team, gear);
+        if (instance.engineOwner == owner && instance.engineProfile != null)
+            instance.engineCue = instance.StartCoroutine(instance.PlayAccelerationCue(owner, instance.engineVersion, cells));
+    }
+
+    private void StartVehicleEngine(MonoBehaviour owner, TeamId team, float completedCells)
+    {
+        StopVehicleEngine();
+        TeamVehicleAudioProfile profile = TeamVehicleAudioRules.GetProfile(team);
+        AudioClip clip = LoadClip(TeamVehicleAudioRules.ValidateResourcePath(profile?.engineResourcePath));
+        if (clip == null)
+            return;
+        AudioClip midClip = LoadClip(TeamVehicleAudioRules.ValidateResourcePath(profile.midEngineResourcePath)) ?? clip;
+        AudioClip highClip = LoadClip(TeamVehicleAudioRules.ValidateResourcePath(profile.highEngineResourcePath)) ?? midClip;
+        engineOwner = owner;
+        engineTeam = team;
+        engineProfile = profile;
+        engineIntensity = TeamVehicleAudioRules.EvaluateIntensity(completedCells, profile.cellsToMax);
+        engineBlendIntensity = engineIntensity;
+        engineTargetPitch = TeamVehicleAudioRules.GetEnginePitch(profile, completedCells);
+        engineEnvelope = 0f;
+        engineSources[0].clip = clip;
+        engineSources[1].clip = midClip;
+        engineSources[2].clip = highClip;
+        // Editor rules/adapter tests may inspect state without starting a real voice.
+        foreach (AudioSource source in engineSources)
+        {
+            source.pitch = engineTargetPitch;
+            source.volume = 0f;
+            if (Application.isPlaying)
+                source.Play();
+        }
+    }
+
+    private IEnumerator PlayAccelerationCue(MonoBehaviour owner, int version, int cells)
+    {
+        float duration = Mathf.Clamp(0.14f + cells * 0.025f, 0.16f, 0.4f);
+        float elapsed = 0f;
+        while (elapsed < duration && version == engineVersion && owner != null && owner.isActiveAndEnabled)
+        {
+            elapsed += Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
+            UpdateVehicleEngine(owner, cells * Mathf.Clamp01(elapsed / duration));
+            yield return null;
+        }
+        if (version != engineVersion)
+            yield break;
+        engineCue = null;
+        EndVehicleEngine(owner);
+    }
+
+    private IEnumerator FadeOutVehicleEngine(int version)
+    {
+        float duration = engineProfile != null && TeamVehicleAudioRules.IsFinite(engineProfile.fadeOutSeconds)
+            ? Mathf.Clamp(engineProfile.fadeOutSeconds, 0.01f, 0.4f) : 0.09f;
+        float from = engineEnvelope;
+        float elapsed = 0f;
+        while (elapsed < duration && version == engineVersion)
+        {
+            elapsed += Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
+            engineEnvelope = Mathf.Lerp(from, 0f, Mathf.Clamp01(elapsed / duration));
+            ApplyEngineVolume();
+            yield return null;
+        }
+        if (version != engineVersion)
+            yield break;
+        engineFade = null;
+        StopVehicleEngine();
+    }
+
+    private void StopVehicleEngine()
+    {
+        engineVersion++;
+        if (engineFade != null)
+            StopCoroutine(engineFade);
+        if (engineCue != null)
+            StopCoroutine(engineCue);
+        engineFade = null;
+        engineCue = null;
+        engineOwner = null;
+        engineProfile = null;
+        engineIntensity = 0f;
+        engineBlendIntensity = 0f;
+        engineEnvelope = 0f;
+        engineEnding = false;
+        engineTargetPitch = 1f;
+        if (engineSources != null)
+        {
+            foreach (AudioSource source in engineSources)
+            {
+                if (source == null) continue;
+                source.Stop();
+                source.clip = null;
+                source.pitch = 1f;
+                source.volume = 0f;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    private void ApplyEngineVolume()
+    {
+        if (engineSources == null)
+            return;
+        float gain = sfxVolume * (engineProfile != null ? SafeGain(engineProfile.engineGain) : 0f) * engineEnvelope;
+        Vector3 weights = TeamVehicleAudioRules.GetEngineLayerWeights(engineBlendIntensity);
+        engineSources[0].volume = gain * weights.x;
+        engineSources[1].volume = gain * weights.y;
+        engineSources[2].volume = gain * weights.z;
+    }
+
+    private static float SafeGain(float value) => TeamVehicleAudioRules.IsFinite(value) ? Mathf.Clamp01(value) : 0f;
+
     private AudioSource CreateSource(string sourceName, bool loop)
     {
         GameObject sourceObject = new GameObject(sourceName);
@@ -111,9 +347,26 @@ public sealed class AudioService : MonoBehaviour
 
     private void OnSceneLoaded(Scene scene, LoadSceneMode mode)
     {
+        if (this == null)
+        {
+            UnsubscribeSceneEvents();
+            return;
+        }
+        StopVehicleEngine();
         PlaySceneMusic(scene.name);
     }
 
+    private void OnSceneUnloaded(Scene scene)
+    {
+        if (this == null)
+        {
+            UnsubscribeSceneEvents();
+            return;
+        }
+        if (engineOwner == null || engineOwner.gameObject.scene == scene)
+            StopVehicleEngine();
+    }
+

     {
         string musicName = AudioRuntimeRules.GetMusicNameForScene(sceneName);

+    /// <summary>
+    /// Recover at most one permanent heat for Fish and Chips: hand first, then discard.
+    /// Temporary heat and the ordinary draw pile are not recovery sources.
+    /// Returns whether a card was removed, including regional heat pending retirement.
+    /// </summary>
+    public bool RecoverOnePermanentHeatFromHandOrDiscard()
+        => RecoverOnePermanentHeatFromPile(hand) || RecoverOnePermanentHeatFromPile(discardPile);
+
+    private bool RecoverOnePermanentHeatFromPile(List<CardData> pile)
+    {
+        for (int i = pile.Count - 1; i >= 0; i--)
+        {
+            CardData card = pile[i];
+            if (card == null || !card.IsHeat || card.isTemp) continue;
+            pile.RemoveAt(i);
+            ReturnPermanentHeatToEngine();
+            return true;
+        }
+        return false;
+    }

+    {

+    }

+    /// <summary>Reads live inputs into an isolated forecast; confirmed ledgers remain authoritative.</summary>
+    public RaceTurnPreview BuildTurnTelemetryPreview(PlayerState player, int? gearOverride = null)
+    {
+        if (player == null || player != Player || player.turnMovement.HasBasePlan || player.isBlown ||
+            player.hasFinished || (CurrentPhase != GamePhase.WaitingForGear && CurrentPhase != GamePhase.WaitingForCards))
+            return null;
+        bool trial = CurrentPhase == GamePhase.WaitingForGear && inputState.WaitingForGear;
+        if (!trial && !inputState.WaitingForCards) return null;
+        IReadOnlyList<CardData> selected = cardHandUI != null ? cardHandUI.GetSelectedPlayCards() : null;
+        int gear = trial ? gearOverride ?? PendingGearForPresentation : player.gear;
+        return RaceTurnPreviewRules.Evaluate(player, session, config, selected, trial, gear,
+            (start, end) => trackManager != null && trackManager.GetUniqueCornersCrossed(start, end).Count > 0,
+            (copy, corner, end) => trackManager != null ? GetNigiriBonus(copy, corner, end, GetLane(player)) : 0,
+            trackManager != null ? trackManager.TotalNodes : 0,
+            (start, end) => trackManager != null ? trackManager.GetUniqueCornersCrossed(start, end) : new HashSet<int>(),
+            id => trackManager != null ? trackManager.GetCornerSpeedLimit(id, GetLane(player)) : 99);
+    }

             : cornerSpeed;

+    /// <summary>
+    /// Read-only card/driver contributions, excluding technology payments and
+    /// consumed bonuses. Return the signed sum; final movement is clamped later.
+    /// </summary>
+    public static int ComputeCardAndDriverMovementBonus(PlayerState player, bool crossedCorner)
+    {
+        int speedPerCardBonus = DriverSkillRules.GetSpeedPerCardBonus(player.driverSkill);
+        int bonus = CardPlayRules.GetHotpotMovementContribution(player, speedPerCardBonus);
+        bonus += player.trickMoveBonusThisTurn;
+        bonus += DriverSkillRules.GetMovementBonus(player.driverSkill, crossedCorner);
+        bonus -= DriverSkillRules.GetGutterMovementPenalty(player.driverSkill);
+        if (player.driverSkill != null)
+            bonus += player.driverSkill.PassiveMovementBonusThisTurn;
+        return bonus;
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Record read-only card/driver values without consuming any effect again.</summary>
+    public static void RecordCardAndDriverMovementPlan(PlayerState player, bool crossedCorner, string trickName = null)
+    {
+        var ledger = player.turnMovement;
+        int index = 0;
+        foreach (var card in player.playedSpeedCardsThisTurn)
+        {
+            int value = CardPlayRules.GetCommittedSpeedCardValue(card);
+            ledger.AddPlan($"速度牌 {++index}（{value}）", value, TurnMovementKind.Card);
+        }
+        string activeName = player.DriverProfile?.ActiveName ?? "车手主动技能";
+        string passiveName = player.DriverProfile?.PassiveName ?? "车手被动技能";
+        ledger.AddPlan($"车手：{activeName}（每张牌）",
+            DriverSkillRules.GetSpeedPerCardBonus(player.driverSkill) * player.playedSpeedCardsThisTurn.Count,
+            TurnMovementKind.Driver);
+        if (player.hotpotAttackAppliedThisTurn)
+            ledger.AddPlan("特技牌：火锅 ATTACK", TrickCardRules.GetHotpotSpeedBonus(), TurnMovementKind.Card);
+        ledger.AddPlan("特技牌：" + (trickName ?? "即时加速"), player.trickMoveBonusThisTurn, TurnMovementKind.Card);
+        ledger.AddPlan("车手：" + activeName, DriverSkillRules.GetMovementBonus(player.driverSkill, crossedCorner), TurnMovementKind.Driver);
+        ledger.AddPlan("车手技能：移动修正", -DriverSkillRules.GetGutterMovementPenalty(player.driverSkill), TurnMovementKind.Driver);
+        if (player.driverSkill != null)
+            ledger.AddPlan("车手：" + passiveName, player.driverSkill.PassiveMovementBonusThisTurn, TurnMovementKind.Driver);
+    }
+
     /// <summary>
     /// Keep the original order: clamp base movement, apply the authored leader
     /// movement, then remove that leader's corner speed for corner resolution.

     {

         // 设置 Play 模式启动场景

@@ -19,4 +23,20 @@ public static class EditorPlayBootstrapper
             EditorSceneManager.playModeStartScene = mainMenuAsset;
         }
     }
+
+    private static void MeasurePlayEntry(PlayModeStateChange state)
+    {
+        if (state == PlayModeStateChange.ExitingEditMode)
+            SessionState.SetString(ENTRY_TIME_KEY, System.DateTime.UtcNow.Ticks.ToString());
+        else if (state == PlayModeStateChange.EnteredPlayMode &&
+                 long.TryParse(SessionState.GetString(ENTRY_TIME_KEY, ""), out long started))
+        {
+            double seconds = (System.DateTime.UtcNow.Ticks - started) / (double)System.TimeSpan.TicksPerSecond;
+            SessionState.SetString(EntrySecondsKey, seconds.ToString("F3", System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
+            UnityEngine.Debug.Log($"[PlayEntry] {seconds:F3}s; domainReloadDisabled=" +
+                (EditorSettings.enterPlayModeOptionsEnabled &&
+                 (EditorSettings.enterPlayModeOptions & EnterPlayModeOptions.DisableDomainReload) != 0));
+            SessionState.EraseString(ENTRY_TIME_KEY);
+        }
+    }
 }

 {
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Dispatches only the authored human UK/JP lesson restrictions. Other
+    /// courses, AI and a missing active step retain ordinary trick selection.
+    /// Existing course-specific validators remain the source of failure text.
+    /// </summary>
+    public static bool ValidateGuidedTrickSelection(
+        bool isAI, string scenarioId, TutorialStepId? step, string trickId, out string reason)
+    {
+        reason = string.Empty;
+        if (isAI || !step.HasValue) return true;
+        if (scenarioId == "tutorial_team_uk_v1")
+            return ValidateUkTrickSelection(step.Value, trickId, out reason);
+        if (scenarioId == "tutorial_team_jp_v1")
+            return ValidateJpTrickSelection(step.Value, trickId, out reason);
+        return true;
+    }

 /// <summary>Pure presentation rules for card-zone transitions.</summary>
 public static class CardZoneTransitionRules
 {
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Routes an actual cooling receipt without changing card ownership.
+    /// Grill keeps its authored hand/draw-to-discard cues before the combined
+    /// discard-to-engine cue. Zero counts are preserved for the FX adapter.
+    /// </summary>
+    public static void PresentHeatCooling(
+        HeatCoolingResult result,
+        bool throughDiscard,
+        System.Action<int, CardVisualZone, CardVisualZone> present)
+    {
+        CardVisualZone destination = throughDiscard ? CardVisualZone.DiscardPile : CardVisualZone.Engine;
+        present(result.FromHand, CardVisualZone.Hand, destination);
+        present(result.FromDraw, CardVisualZone.DrawPile, destination);
+        present(throughDiscard ? result.Total : result.FromDiscard,
+            CardVisualZone.DiscardPile, CardVisualZone.Engine);
+    }

+    [TestCase(1, 0)]

+        deck.AddCardsToHand(new[] { temporary, trick });
+        // Inject a legacy draw-pile heat: this is explicitly NOT a Fish recovery source.
+        var draw = (List<CardData>)typeof(CardDeck).GetField("drawPile",
+            System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Instance | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic).GetValue(deck);
+        var legacyHeat = new CardData(CardType.Heat, 0);
+        draw.Add(legacyHeat);
+        int poolBefore = deck.heatPool.remaining;
+        int permanentBefore = poolBefore + deck.CountPermanentHeatOutsideEngine();
+        bool expected = handHeat + discardHeat > 0;
+
+        Assert.AreEqual(expected, deck.RecoverOnePermanentHeatFromHandOrDiscard());
+        if (handHeat > 0) handBefore.RemoveAt(handBefore.Count - 1);
+        else if (discardHeat > 0) discardBefore.RemoveAt(discardBefore.Count - 1);
+        handBefore.Add(temporary);
+        handBefore.Add(trick);
+        CollectionAssert.AreEqual(handBefore, deck.Hand);
+        CollectionAssert.AreEqual(discardBefore, deck.DiscardPile);
+        CollectionAssert.AreEqual(new[] { speed, legacyHeat }, deck.DrawPile);
+        Assert.AreEqual(poolBefore + (expected ? 1 : 0), deck.heatPool.remaining);
+        Assert.AreEqual(permanentBefore, deck.heatPool.remaining + deck.CountPermanentHeatOutsideEngine());
+
+        int remaining = handHeat + discardHeat - (expected ? 1 : 0);
+        for (int i = 0; i < remaining; i++)
+            Assert.IsTrue(deck.RecoverOnePermanentHeatFromHandOrDiscard());
+        Assert.IsFalse(deck.RecoverOnePermanentHeatFromHandOrDiscard());
+        Assert.AreEqual(4, deck.heatPool.remaining, "Temporary/legacy heat cannot inflate the engine.");
+        CollectionAssert.AreEqual(new[] { temporary, trick }, deck.Hand);
+        Assert.IsEmpty(deck.DiscardPile);
+        CollectionAssert.AreEqual(new[] { speed, legacyHeat }, deck.DrawPile);
+    }

+    [TestCase(0, 0, 0, false)]
+    [TestCase(1, 0, 0, false)]
+    [TestCase(0, 2, 0, false)]
+    [TestCase(0, 0, 3, false)]
+    [TestCase(2, 3, 4, false)]
+    [TestCase(0, 0, 0, true)]
+    [TestCase(1, 0, 0, true)]
+    [TestCase(0, 2, 0, true)]
+    [TestCase(0, 0, 3, true)]
+    [TestCase(2, 3, 4, true)]
+    public void CoolingRoutesPreserveLegacyOrderCountsAndDestination(
+        int hand, int draw, int discard, bool throughDiscard)
+    {
+        var calls = new System.Collections.Generic.List<(int, CardVisualZone, CardVisualZone)>();
+        var receipt = new HeatCoolingResult(hand, draw, discard);
+        CardZoneTransitionRules.PresentHeatCooling(receipt, throughDiscard,
+            (count, source, destination) => calls.Add((count, source, destination)));
+
+        CardVisualZone target = throughDiscard ? CardVisualZone.DiscardPile : CardVisualZone.Engine;
+        Assert.That(calls, Is.EqualTo(new[]
+        {
+            (hand, CardVisualZone.Hand, target),
+            (draw, CardVisualZone.DrawPile, target),
+            (throughDiscard ? hand + draw + discard : discard,
+                CardVisualZone.DiscardPile, CardVisualZone.Engine)
+        }));
+        Assert.That((receipt.FromHand, receipt.FromDraw, receipt.FromDiscard),
+            Is.EqualTo((hand, draw, discard)));
+    }

+    [TestCase(0)]
+    [TestCase(1)]
+    [TestCase(11)]
+    public void ConsecutiveRebuildsRetireOnlyThePreviousGeneration(int count)
+    {
+        var bindings = (Dictionary<PlayerState, AIController>)Get("aiControllers");
+        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
+            bindings.Add(new PlayerState("initial-" + i, true, 0, 1), root.AddComponent<AIController>());
+        var retired = new HashSet<AIController>();
+        for (int generation = 0; generation < 3; generation++)
+        {
+            var previous = bindings.Values.ToArray();
+            var next = new PlayerState("generation-" + generation, true, 0, 1);
+            var trace = new List<string>();
+            Rebuild(controller =>
+            {
+                Assert.That(previous, Does.Contain(controller));
+                Assert.That(retired.Add(controller), Is.True, "No generation is retired twice.");
+                trace.Add("retire");
+                UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(controller);
+            }, () =>
+            {
+                Assert.That(bindings, Is.Empty);
+                Assert.That(root.GetComponents<AIController>(), Is.Empty);
+                bindings.Add(next, root.AddComponent<AIController>());
+                trace.Add("initialize");
+            }, () => trace.Add("guide"), () => trace.Add("loop"));
+            Assert.That(trace, Is.EqualTo(Enumerable.Repeat("retire", previous.Length)
+                .Concat(new[] { "initialize", "guide", "loop" })));
+            Assert.That(bindings.Keys, Is.EqualTo(new[] { next }));
+            Assert.That(root.GetComponents<AIController>(), Is.EqualTo(bindings.Values.ToArray()));
+        }
+        Assert.That(retired.Count, Is.EqualTo(count + 2));
+    }
+
+    [TestCase(0)]
+    [TestCase(1)]
+    [TestCase(2)]
+    [TestCase(3)]
+    public void FailedRebuildCanBeFollowedByFreshOwnedBindingsWithoutRollback(int failureStage)
+    {
+        var bindings = (Dictionary<PlayerState, AIController>)Get("aiControllers");
+        var old = root.AddComponent<AIController>();
+        bindings.Add(new PlayerState("old", true, 0, 1), old);
+        var failedGeneration = new PlayerState("failed generation", true, 0, 1);
+        var failure = new InvalidOperationException("fixture generation failure");
+        Assert.That(Assert.Throws<TargetInvocationException>(() => Rebuild(
+            controller =>
+            {
+                if (failureStage == 0) throw failure;
+                UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(controller);
+            }, () =>
+            {
+                bindings.Add(failedGeneration, root.AddComponent<AIController>());
+                if (failureStage == 1) throw failure;
+            }, () => { if (failureStage == 2) throw failure; },
+            () => { if (failureStage == 3) throw failure; })).InnerException, Is.SameAs(failure));
+        var remnants = root.GetComponents<AIController>();
+        Assert.That(remnants.Length, Is.EqualTo(1));
+        var fresh = new PlayerState("fresh", true, 0, 1);
+        var retired = new List<AIController>();
+        int guideCalls = 0, loopCalls = 0;
+        Rebuild(controller =>
+        {
+            retired.Add(controller);
+            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(controller);
+        }, () =>
+        {
+            Assert.That(bindings, Is.Empty);
+            Assert.That(root.GetComponents<AIController>(), Is.Empty);
+            bindings.Add(fresh, root.AddComponent<AIController>());
+        }, () => guideCalls++, () => loopCalls++);
+        Assert.That(retired, Is.EqualTo(remnants));
+        Assert.That(bindings.Keys, Is.EqualTo(new[] { fresh }));
+        Assert.That(root.GetComponents<AIController>(), Is.EqualTo(bindings.Values.ToArray()));
+        Assert.That(guideCalls, Is.EqualTo(1));
+        Assert.That(loopCalls, Is.EqualTo(1));
+    }

+    [TestCase(3)] [TestCase(5)] [TestCase(7)]
+    public void ReactionHeatCostMatchesLegacyGateWithoutConsumingAnyDriverState(int level)
+    {
+        Assert.That(DriverSkillRules.GetReactionHeatCost(null), Is.Zero);
+        foreach (DriverProfile driver in DriverCatalog.All)
+        {
+            var state = new DriverSkillRuntimeState();
+            state.Initialize(driver, level, true);
+            Assert.That(DriverSkillRules.GetReactionHeatCost(state), Is.Zero, driver.Id);
+            Assert.That(state.TryActivate(driver,
+                new DriverSkillActivationContext(true, 2, 3, 1, 10, 3), out string reason), Is.True, reason);
+            int uses = state.UsesRemaining, turns = state.ActiveTurnsRemaining;
+            int expected = state.IsActive && state.Skill == DriverActiveSkillId.TrueSelf ? 1 : 0;
+            for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
+                Assert.That(DriverSkillRules.GetReactionHeatCost(state), Is.EqualTo(expected), driver.Id);
+            Assert.That(state.UsesRemaining, Is.EqualTo(uses));
+            Assert.That(state.ActiveTurnsRemaining, Is.EqualTo(turns));
+            Assert.That(state.ActivatedThisTurn, Is.True);
+            state.Initialize(driver, level, false);
+            Assert.That(DriverSkillRules.GetReactionHeatCost(state), Is.Zero);
+        }
+    }

     private object Invoke(string name, params object[] args) =>
         typeof(MVPGameManager).GetMethod(name, Private).Invoke(manager, args);
 
+    [TestCase(TeamId.CN, false)]
+    [TestCase(TeamId.CN, true)]
+    [TestCase(TeamId.US, false)]
+    [TestCase(TeamId.US, true)]
+    public void AuthoredShanghaiChoiceWaitResumesIntoReservationAndNextTurn(TeamId team, bool enter)
+    {
+        // Exercise the real waiting iterator and public callback without starting
+        // a scene, GameLoop, pause menu, tutorial, or persistent reward storage.
+        var authored = TrackDataLoader.LoadConfig("shanghai_dim_sum");
+        Assert.IsNotNull(authored);
+        var nodes = TrackDataLoader.ConfigToNodes(authored);
+        Assert.IsTrue(PitLaneRules.HasPitLane(nodes));
+        int entry = PitLaneRules.FindPitEntry(nodes);
+        int exit = PitLaneRules.FindPitExit(nodes);
+        typeof(TrackManager).GetField("nodes", Private).SetValue(manager.trackManager, nodes);
+        SetField("tutorialPitRuleNodes", null); // Use actual TrackManager data, not tutorial fallback.
+        var config = ScriptableObject.CreateInstance<GameConfigSO>();
+        try
+        {
+            config.enablePitLane = true;
+            config.enableTechTree = false;
+            config.minGear = 2;
+            config.pitExitMoveBonus = 1;
+            manager.config = config;
+            var session = new RaceSession(new SystemRandomSource(1));
+            SetField("session", session);
+            player = RacePitStopExecutionTests.Player(team);
+            player.position = (entry - 1 + nodes.Count) % nodes.Count;
+            player.pitStopScheduled = player.skipNextTurn = false;
+            player.pitStopRequested = player.pitChoiceResolvedThisLap = false;
+            SetField("pitWaitingPlayer", player);
+            session.Players.Add(player);
+            var panel = new GameObject("Isolated pit choice panel");
+            panel.transform.SetParent(host.transform, false);
+            panel.SetActive(false);
+            SetField("pitChoicePanel", panel);
+            Assert.IsTrue(PitLaneRules.TryGetApproachChoiceDistance(player, nodes, true, out int distance));
+            Assert.AreEqual(1, distance);
+
+            var routine = (IEnumerator)Invoke("WaitForPitChoice", distance);
+            Assert.IsTrue(routine.MoveNext());
+            Assert.IsTrue(panel.activeSelf);
+            Assert.IsTrue(input.WaitingForPitChoice);
+            var wait = routine.Current as WaitWhile;
+            Assert.IsNotNull(wait);
+            // Polling the suspended wait must not reserve, move, cool, or advance the turn.
+            for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) Assert.IsTrue(wait.keepWaiting);
+            Assert.IsFalse(player.pitStopRequested);
+            Assert.AreEqual(2, player.deck.heatPool.remaining);
+            Assert.AreEqual(4, player.heatPaidCardsThisTurn.Count);
+            int start = player.position;
+            manager.ChoosePit(enter);
+            manager.ChoosePit(!enter); // Late/double input cannot reverse the accepted decision.
+            Assert.IsFalse(wait.keepWaiting);
+            Assert.IsFalse(routine.MoveNext());
+            Assert.IsFalse(panel.activeSelf);
+            Assert.IsFalse(input.WaitingForPitChoice);
+            Assert.AreEqual(start, player.position);
+            Assert.AreEqual(enter, player.pitStopRequested);
+            Assert.IsFalse(player.pitStopScheduled);
+
+            player.position = (start + 2) % nodes.Count; // Settled movement crosses the authored entry.
+            Invoke("RegisterPitEntryCrossing", player, start, start + 2);
+            Assert.AreEqual(enter, player.pitStopScheduled);
+            Assert.IsFalse(player.pitStopRequested);
+            session.BeginTurn(player);
+            Assert.AreEqual(enter ? RaceTurnStartAction.ExecuteScheduledPitStop : RaceTurnStartAction.SelectGear,
+                RaceTurnRules.GetStartAction(player));
+            if (enter)
+            {
+                Invoke("ExecuteScheduledPitStop", player);
+                Assert.AreEqual((exit + 1) % nodes.Count, player.position);
+                Assert.AreEqual(team == TeamId.CN ? 1 : 2, player.gear);
+                Assert.AreEqual(6, player.deck.heatPool.remaining);
+                Assert.AreEqual(0, player.deck.CountPermanentHeatOutsideEngine());
+            }
+            else
+            {
+                Assert.AreEqual((start + 2) % nodes.Count, player.position);
+                Assert.AreEqual(4, player.gear);
+                Assert.AreEqual(2, player.deck.heatPool.remaining);
+                Assert.AreEqual(4, player.deck.CountPermanentHeatOutsideEngine());
+            }
+            Assert.AreEqual(2, player.lap); // Stop/crossing adapters must not invent lap completion.
+            Assert.IsFalse(player.pitStopScheduled);
+            Assert.IsFalse(player.skipNextTurn);
+            Assert.IsFalse(player.pitChoiceResolvedThisLap);
+            session.BeginTurn(player);
+            Assert.AreEqual(RaceTurnStartAction.SelectGear, RaceTurnRules.GetStartAction(player));
+            player.position = start + nodes.Count;
+            Assert.IsTrue(PitLaneRules.TryGetApproachChoiceDistance(player, nodes, true, out distance));
+            Assert.AreEqual(1, distance);
+        }
+        finally
+        {
+            manager.config = null;
+            Object.DestroyImmediate(config);
+        }
+    }

+        bool enter, bool confirmationEnabled)
+    {
+        const BindingFlags StaticPrivate = BindingFlags.Static | BindingFlags.NonPublic;
+        FieldInfo settings = typeof(GameSettingsRuntime).GetField("current", StaticPrivate);
+        FieldInfo repository = typeof(GameSettingsRuntime).GetField("repository", StaticPrivate);
+        FieldInfo audio = typeof(AudioService).GetField("instance", StaticPrivate);
+        object previousSettings = settings.GetValue(null);
+        object previousRepository = repository.GetValue(null);
+        object previousAudio = audio.GetValue(null);
+        try
+        {
+            // Replace only the in-memory settings view; never save preferences or change display/audio.
+            settings.SetValue(null, new GameSettingsData
+            {
+                inRaceConfirmationMask = confirmationEnabled ? (int)InRaceConfirmationAction.PitDecision : 0
+            });
+            audio.SetValue(null, null);
+            using (var scope = CareerUiPointerTestScope.Create(() => AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<TMP_FontAsset>(
+                "Assets/TmpFont/StreamingAssets/Fonts & Materials/Chinese/SourceHanSansTC-Medium-HQ.asset")))
+            {
+                GameObject hudHost = new GameObject("Pit pointer HUD", typeof(RectTransform));
+                hudHost.SetActive(false);
+                hudHost.transform.SetParent(scope.Root.transform, false);
+                HUDUI hud = hudHost.AddComponent<HUDUI>();
+                manager.hudUI = hud;
+                Invoke("CreatePitChoiceUI");
+                hud.EnsureReturnToMenuConfirmationUI();
+                // Production Start binds these listeners; avoid starting an unrelated HUD lifecycle.
+                hud.cancelReturnToMenuButton.onClick.AddListener(hud.CancelReturnToMenu);
+                hud.confirmReturnToMenuButton.onClick.AddListener(hud.ConfirmReturnToMenu);
+                foreach (TMP_Text label in scope.Root.GetComponentsInChildren<TMP_Text>(true))
+                    label.font = scope.Font;
+
+                var routine = (IEnumerator)Invoke("WaitForPitChoice", 1);
+                Assert.IsTrue(routine.MoveNext());
+                var wait = (WaitWhile)routine.Current;
+                Button selected = (Button)typeof(MVPGameManager).GetField(
+                    enter ? "pitEnterButton" : "pitSkipButton", Private).GetValue(manager);
+                Button opposite = (Button)typeof(MVPGameManager).GetField(
+                    enter ? "pitSkipButton" : "pitEnterButton", Private).GetValue(manager);
+                scope.Click(selected);
+                if (confirmationEnabled)
+                {
+                    Assert.IsTrue(hud.IsReturnToMenuConfirmationVisible);
+                    Assert.IsTrue(wait.keepWaiting);
+                    Assert.IsFalse(player.pitChoiceResolvedThisLap);
+                    scope.Click(hud.cancelReturnToMenuButton);
+                    Assert.IsFalse(hud.IsReturnToMenuConfirmationVisible);
+                    Assert.IsTrue(wait.keepWaiting);
+                    Assert.IsFalse(player.pitStopRequested);
+                    scope.Click(selected);
+                    scope.Click(hud.confirmReturnToMenuButton);
+                }
+                Assert.IsFalse(hud.IsReturnToMenuConfirmationVisible);
+                Assert.IsFalse(wait.keepWaiting);
+                Assert.IsTrue(player.pitChoiceResolvedThisLap);
+                Assert.AreEqual(enter, player.pitStopRequested);
+                Assert.IsFalse(player.pitStopScheduled);
+                Assert.AreEqual(9, player.position);
+
+                // A second geometry-verified click before the waiter hides the panel cannot reverse settlement.
+                scope.Click(opposite);
+                if (confirmationEnabled) scope.Click(hud.confirmReturnToMenuButton);
+                Assert.AreEqual(enter, player.pitStopRequested);
+                Assert.IsFalse(input.WaitingForPitChoice);
+                Assert.IsFalse(routine.MoveNext());
+                Assert.IsFalse(selected.gameObject.activeInHierarchy);
+            }
+        }
+        finally
+        {
+            manager.hudUI = null;
+            settings.SetValue(null, previousSettings);
+            repository.SetValue(null, previousRepository);
+            audio.SetValue(null, previousAudio);
+        }
+    }

+    internal static System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable<string> CaseResultKeys => caseKeys.Values;
+
+    internal static string ResolveCaseKey(string fullName) =>
+        fullName != null && caseKeys.TryGetValue(fullName, out string key) ? key : null;
+
+    // Lazily serialize recognized cases only. Preserve failures as failures;
+    // this boundary neither interprets nor invents a result payload.
+    internal static void CaptureCaseResult(string fullName, Func<string> serialize, Action<string, string> write)
+    {
+        string key = ResolveCaseKey(fullName);
+        if (key != null)
+            write(key, serialize());
+    }

+    // Re-arming after a domain reload must retain evidence from this run.
+    // Inject only the erase operation so guards never touch live SessionState.
+    internal static void ClearStaleResults(bool alreadyArmed, Action<string> erase)
+    {
+        if (alreadyArmed) return;
+        erase(RunResultKey);
+        foreach (string key in CaseResultKeys) erase(key);
+    }

+/// <summary>Pure registry guards; never arm callbacks or mutate stored evidence.</summary>
+public class RaceCoroutineEvidenceRegistryTests

+    private sealed class SummarySnapshot
+    {
+        public string fullName, resultState, endedUtc, message, output;
+        public int total, passed, failed, skipped, inconclusive;

+        public DateTime StartTime => EndTime.AddSeconds(-1);
+        public int PassCount => 1;
+        public int FailCount => 2;
+        public int SkipCount => 3;
+        public int InconclusiveCount => 4;
+        public string Message => "message \"quoted\"\n第二行";
+        public string Output => "raw output\nnot a success verdict";
+        public ITestAdaptor Test => throw new NotSupportedException();
+        public UnityEditor.TestTools.TestRunner.Api.TestStatus TestStatus => throw new NotSupportedException();
+        public double Duration => 1;
+        public string StackTrace => "fixture stack";
+        public int AssertCount => 0;
+        public bool HasChildren => false;
+        public System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable<ITestResultAdaptor> Children => Array.Empty<ITestResultAdaptor>();
+        public NUnit.Framework.Interfaces.TNode ToXml() => throw new NotSupportedException();
+    }

+    [NUnit.Framework.TestCase("Passed", true)]
+    [NUnit.Framework.TestCase("Failed:Error", true)]
+    [NUnit.Framework.TestCase("Skipped:Ignored", true)]
+    [NUnit.Framework.TestCase("Inconclusive", true)]
+    [NUnit.Framework.TestCase("Passed", false)]
+    [NUnit.Framework.TestCase("Failed:Cancelled", false)]
+    public void SummarySerializationPreservesNativeFactsAndIncludesOutputOnlyForCases(string state, bool includeOutput)
+    {
+        var result = new ResultFixture { ResultState = state };
+        string json = RaceCoroutineTestEvidence.SerializeResult(result, includeOutput);
+        SummarySnapshot summary = JsonUtility.FromJson<SummarySnapshot>(json);
+        NUnit.Framework.Assert.That(summary.fullName, NUnit.Framework.Is.EqualTo(result.FullName));
+        NUnit.Framework.Assert.That(summary.resultState, NUnit.Framework.Is.EqualTo(state));
+        NUnit.Framework.Assert.That(summary.endedUtc, NUnit.Framework.Is.EqualTo("2026-10-12T00:35:00.0000000Z"));
+        NUnit.Framework.Assert.That(summary.total, NUnit.Framework.Is.EqualTo(10));
+        NUnit.Framework.Assert.That(new[] { summary.passed, summary.failed, summary.skipped, summary.inconclusive },
+            NUnit.Framework.Is.EqualTo(new[] { 1, 2, 3, 4 }));
+        NUnit.Framework.Assert.That(summary.message, NUnit.Framework.Is.EqualTo(result.Message));
+        NUnit.Framework.Assert.That(summary.output, NUnit.Framework.Is.EqualTo(includeOutput ? result.Output : string.Empty));
+    }

+    {
+        const string name = "RaceGameLoopCoroutineTests.RealCareerFinaleCompletesStandingsAndRejectsNinthRace";
+        var storage = new System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<string, string>
+        { { RaceCoroutineTestEvidence.RunResultKey, "run" }, { "unrelated", "keep" } };
+        foreach (string payload in new[] { "failed", "passed" })
+            RaceCoroutineTestEvidence.CaptureCaseResult(name, () => payload, (key, value) => storage[key] = value);
+        NUnit.Framework.Assert.That(storage.Count, NUnit.Framework.Is.EqualTo(3));
+        NUnit.Framework.Assert.That(storage[RaceCoroutineTestEvidence.CareerFinaleCaseResultKey], NUnit.Framework.Is.EqualTo("passed"));
+        NUnit.Framework.Assert.That(storage[RaceCoroutineTestEvidence.RunResultKey], NUnit.Framework.Is.EqualTo("run"));
+        NUnit.Framework.Assert.That(storage["unrelated"], NUnit.Framework.Is.EqualTo("keep"));
+    }

+    [NUnit.Framework.Test]
+    public void FirstArmClearsEveryStaleResultOnceButPreservesOtherStorage()
+    {
+        var storage = RaceCoroutineTestEvidence.CaseResultKeys.ToDictionary(key => key, key => "stale");
+        storage.Add(RaceCoroutineTestEvidence.RunResultKey, "stale run");
+        storage.Add("unrelated", "keep");
+        var erased = new System.Collections.Generic.List<string>();
+
+        RaceCoroutineTestEvidence.ClearStaleResults(false, key => { erased.Add(key); storage.Remove(key); });
+
+        int expectedCount = RaceCoroutineTestEvidence.CaseResultKeys.Count() + 1;
+        NUnit.Framework.Assert.That(erased.Count, NUnit.Framework.Is.EqualTo(expectedCount));
+        NUnit.Framework.Assert.That(erased.Distinct().Count(), NUnit.Framework.Is.EqualTo(expectedCount));
+        NUnit.Framework.Assert.That(erased[0], NUnit.Framework.Is.EqualTo(RaceCoroutineTestEvidence.RunResultKey));
+        NUnit.Framework.CollectionAssert.AreEquivalent(RaceCoroutineTestEvidence.CaseResultKeys, erased.Skip(1));
+        NUnit.Framework.Assert.That(storage.Count, NUnit.Framework.Is.EqualTo(1));
+        NUnit.Framework.Assert.That(storage["unrelated"], NUnit.Framework.Is.EqualTo("keep"));
+    }

+    [NUnit.Framework.Test]
+    public void RepeatedArmRetainsFreshResultsUntilTheNextRun()
+    {
+        var storage = new System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<string, string>();
+        Action<string> erase = key => storage.Remove(key);
+        RaceCoroutineTestEvidence.ClearStaleResults(false, erase);
+        storage.Add(RaceCoroutineTestEvidence.RunResultKey, "fresh run");
+        foreach (string key in RaceCoroutineTestEvidence.CaseResultKeys) storage.Add(key, "fresh case");
+        var fresh = new System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<string, string>(storage);
+
+        RaceCoroutineTestEvidence.ClearStaleResults(true, erase);
+        NUnit.Framework.CollectionAssert.AreEquivalent(fresh, storage);
+        RaceCoroutineTestEvidence.ClearStaleResults(false, erase);
+        NUnit.Framework.Assert.That(storage, NUnit.Framework.Is.Empty);
+    }

+    [NUnit.Framework.Test]
+    public void CleanupIncludesEveryCaseConstantOnceAndExcludesRunSummary()
+    {
+        var expected = System.Linq.Enumerable.Where(typeof(RaceCoroutineTestEvidence).GetFields(
+            System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Static | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic),
+            field => field.IsLiteral && field.Name.EndsWith("CaseResultKey", StringComparison.Ordinal)
+                || field.IsLiteral && field.Name == "CaseResultKey");
+        var keys = new System.Collections.Generic.List<string>(RaceCoroutineTestEvidence.CaseResultKeys);
+        NUnit.Framework.CollectionAssert.AreEquivalent(System.Linq.Enumerable.Select(expected,
+            field => (string)field.GetRawConstantValue()), keys);
+        NUnit.Framework.Assert.That(System.Linq.Enumerable.Distinct(keys).Count(), NUnit.Framework.Is.EqualTo(keys.Count));
+        NUnit.Framework.Assert.That(keys, NUnit.Framework.Does.Not.Contain(RaceCoroutineTestEvidence.RunResultKey));
+    }

 public class RaceLaneRulesTests
 {
+    [TestCase(2)]
+    [TestCase(4)]
+    [TestCase(6)]
+    [TestCase(12)]
+    public void SeededRostersMatchActivePrefixOccupancyWithoutMutatingPlayers(int count)
+    {
+        var random = new System.Random(7331 + count);
+        for (int trial = 0; trial < 64; trial++)
+        {
+            var players = new List<PlayerState>();
+            for (int index = 0; index < count; index++)
+            {
+                int status = random.Next(4);
+                players.Add(random.Next(7) == 0 ? null : new PlayerState(
+                    "Car" + index, index != 0, random.Next(4), 1)
+                {
+                    lap = random.Next(1, 4),
+                    hasFinished = status == 2,
+                    isBlown = status == 3
+                });
+            }
+
+            var references = players.ToArray();
+            var snapshots = players.ConvertAll(player => player == null
+                ? (0, 0, false, false)
+                : (player.lap, player.position, player.hasFinished, player.isBlown));
+            // Independent oracle: accumulate cells claimed by the active prefix,
+            // rather than repeating the production method's per-candidate scan.
+            var occupied = new HashSet<(int, int)>();
+            for (int index = 0; index < count; index++)
+            {
+                PlayerState player = players[index];
+                bool active = player != null && !player.hasFinished && !player.isBlown;
+                bool expected = active && occupied.Contains((player.lap, player.position));
+                Assert.That(RaceLaneRules.IsTrailingInParallel(player, players, index),
+                    Is.EqualTo(expected), "trial=" + trial + ", index=" + index);
+                if (active) occupied.Add((player.lap, player.position));
+            }
+
+            for (int index = 0; index < count; index++)
+            {
+                Assert.That(players[index], Is.SameAs(references[index]));
+                if (players[index] == null) continue;
+                PlayerState player = players[index];
+                Assert.That((player.lap, player.position, player.hasFinished, player.isBlown),
+                    Is.EqualTo(snapshots[index]));
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    [Test]
+    public void RepeatedCandidateReferenceDoesNotClaimItsOwnCell()
+    {
+        var candidate = new PlayerState("Candidate", true, 8, 1) { lap = 1 };
+        var players = new List<PlayerState> { candidate, null, candidate };
+        Assert.That(RaceLaneRules.IsTrailingInParallel(candidate, players, 2), Is.False);
+
+        players.Insert(1, new PlayerState("Distinct", false, 8, 1) { lap = 1 });
+        Assert.That(RaceLaneRules.IsTrailingInParallel(candidate, players, 3), Is.True);
+    }
+
+    [TestCase(false, false)]
+    [TestCase(true, false)]
+    [TestCase(false, true)]
+    [TestCase(true, true)]
+    public void TerminalFlagsExcludeBothClaimantAndCandidate(bool finished, bool blown)
+    {
+        var earlier = new PlayerState("Earlier", false, 8, 1) { lap = 1 };
+        var candidate = new PlayerState("Candidate", true, 8, 1) { lap = 1 };
+        var players = new List<PlayerState> { earlier, candidate };
+        earlier.hasFinished = finished;
+        earlier.isBlown = blown;
+        Assert.That(RaceLaneRules.IsTrailingInParallel(candidate, players, 1),
+            Is.EqualTo(!finished && !blown));
+
+        earlier.hasFinished = false;
+        earlier.isBlown = false;
+        candidate.hasFinished = finished;
+        candidate.isBlown = blown;
+        Assert.That(RaceLaneRules.IsTrailingInParallel(candidate, players, 1),
+            Is.EqualTo(!finished && !blown));
+    }
+
+    [Test]
+    public void LaterMatchingCarDoesNotChangeEarlierCandidate()
+    {
+        var candidate = new PlayerState("Candidate", true, 8, 1) { lap = 1 };
+        var later = new PlayerState("Later", false, 8, 1) { lap = 1 };
+        Assert.That(RaceLaneRules.IsTrailingInParallel(candidate,
+            new List<PlayerState> { null, candidate, later }, 1), Is.False);
+    }

+    [TestCaseSource(nameof(CardDriverBonusCases))]
+    public void CardDriverSumPreservesLegacyOrderAndDoesNotConsumeState(string driverId, bool crossedCorner)
+    {
+        Assert.IsTrue(DriverCatalog.TryGet(driverId, out DriverProfile driver));
+        var state = new DriverSkillRuntimeState();
+        state.Initialize(driver, 7, true);
+        Assert.IsTrue(state.TryActivate(driver,
+            new DriverSkillActivationContext(true, 2, 3, 1, 10, 3), out string reason), reason);
+        var player = new PlayerState("movement", false, 9, 2)
+        {
+            driverSkill = state, hotpotAttackAppliedThisTurn = true,
+            hotpotAttackCardValueThisTurn = 4, trickMoveBonusThisTurn = -2
+        };
+        int expected = CardPlayRules.GetHotpotMovementContribution(player,
+            DriverSkillRules.GetSpeedPerCardBonus(state));
+        expected += player.trickMoveBonusThisTurn;
+        expected += DriverSkillRules.GetMovementBonus(state, crossedCorner);
+        expected -= DriverSkillRules.GetGutterMovementPenalty(state);
+        expected += state.PassiveMovementBonusThisTurn;
+        int uses = state.UsesRemaining, duration = state.ActiveTurnsRemaining;
+        for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
+            Assert.AreEqual(expected, RaceMovementRules.ComputeCardAndDriverMovementBonus(player, crossedCorner));
+        Assert.AreEqual(uses, state.UsesRemaining);
+        Assert.AreEqual(duration, state.ActiveTurnsRemaining);
+        Assert.IsTrue(state.ActivatedThisTurn);
+        Assert.IsTrue(player.hotpotAttackAppliedThisTurn);
+        Assert.AreEqual(4, player.hotpotAttackCardValueThisTurn);
+        Assert.AreEqual(-2, player.trickMoveBonusThisTurn);
+        Assert.AreEqual(9, player.position);
+        Assert.AreEqual(2, player.gear);
+    }

+    [Test]
+    public void CardDriverSumReadsLivePassiveRewardWithoutConsumingOrCachingIt()
+    {
+        Assert.IsTrue(DriverCatalog.TryGet("uk_nigel_mansell", out DriverProfile driver));
+        var state = new DriverSkillRuntimeState();
+        state.Initialize(driver, 7, true);
+        state.ResolvePassiveTurnEnd(1);
+        state.BeginTurn();
+        int passive = state.PassiveMovementBonusThisTurn;
+        Assert.Greater(passive, 0);
+        var player = new PlayerState("passive", false, 0, 1) { driverSkill = state };
+        Assert.AreEqual(passive, RaceMovementRules.ComputeCardAndDriverMovementBonus(player, false));
+        Assert.AreEqual(passive, RaceMovementRules.ComputeCardAndDriverMovementBonus(player, true));
+        Assert.AreEqual(passive, state.PassiveMovementBonusThisTurn);
+        player.hotpotAttackAppliedThisTurn = true;
+        player.hotpotAttackCardValueThisTurn = 4;
+        Assert.AreEqual(passive + 5, RaceMovementRules.ComputeCardAndDriverMovementBonus(player, false));
+        state.BeginTurn();
+        Assert.AreEqual(0, state.PassiveMovementBonusThisTurn);
+        Assert.AreEqual(5, RaceMovementRules.ComputeCardAndDriverMovementBonus(player, false));
+    }

+    private CardData[] ShowExactHand(int count)
+    {
+        var cards = new CardData[count];
+        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) cards[i] = new CardData(CardType.Speed, 3);

+        manager.cardHandUI.cardPrefab = template;

+        manager.cardHandUI.ShowHand(manager, player);

+        Assert.AreEqual(6, player.deck.heatPool.remaining);
+
+        manager.OnPlayCardsButtonClicked();
+        Assert.IsFalse(input.WaitingForCards);
+        manager.OnPlayCardsButtonClicked();
+        Assert.AreEqual(count, player.playedSpeedCardsThisTurn.Count);

+    {

+        else if (phase == GamePhase.Animating) Phase.BeginAnimation();
+        else Phase.CompleteGame();
+
+        manager.OnPlayCardsButtonClicked();
+
+        Assert.IsTrue(input.WaitingForCards);
+        Assert.AreEqual(phase, Phase.Current);
+        Assert.AreEqual(6, player.deck.heatPool.remaining);
+        Assert.AreEqual(0, player.deck.CountHeatInHand());

+    [TestCase("normal")]
+    [TestCase("tutorial")]
+    [TestCase("career")]
+    public void real_ranking_formats_only_standings_and_preserves_mode_copy(string mode)
+    {
+        var players = new List<PlayerState>();
+        for (int i = 12; i >= 1; i--)
+            players.Add(new PlayerState("ResultDriver" + i, i != 1, i, 1)
+            { teamId = TeamId.UK, lap = 3, hasFinished = i != 12, isBlown = i == 12, finishOrder = i });
+        string ranking = RaceRanking.FormatResults(players);
+        string suffix = "\n\n1. 奖励说明\n2. 存档说明";
+        string raw = mode == "tutorial" ? TutorialRaceResultPresentation.BuildSummary(ranking, false) + suffix :
+            mode == "career" ? ranking + CareerRaceResultPresentation.BuildRejectedSummary("test rejection") + suffix :
+            ranking + suffix;
+
+        string formatted = RaceResultPresentationRules.FormatForRichText(raw);
+
+        Assert.That(formatted, Does.Not.Contain("=== 比赛结果 ==="));
+        string[] rankedLines = ranking.Split('\n');
+        for (int rank = 1; rank <= 12; rank++)
+        {
+            string line = rankedLines[rank + 1];
+            string expected = rank == 1 ? "<color=#FFD75A><b>【冠军】 " + line + "</b></color>" :
+                rank == 2 ? "<color=#DDE7F2><b>【亚军】 " + line + "</b></color>" :
+                rank == 3 ? "<color=#E7A56A><b>【季军】 " + line + "</b></color>" :
+                "<color=#C2CDDD>" + line + "</color>";
+            Assert.That(formatted, Does.Contain(expected));
+        }
+        Assert.That(formatted, Does.EndWith(suffix));
+        Assert.That(formatted, Does.Not.Contain("【冠军】 1. 奖励说明"));
+        if (mode == "tutorial") Assert.That(formatted, Does.Contain("本次练习未完成"));
+        if (mode == "career") Assert.That(formatted, Does.Contain("test rejection"));
+        Assert.That(players[0].name, Is.EqualTo("ResultDriver12"), "Formatting must not sort the source roster in place.");
+        Assert.That(players[0].isBlown, Is.True);
+        Assert.That(players[11].finishOrder, Is.EqualTo(1));
+    }
+
+    [TestCase(2)]
+    [TestCase(4)]
+    public void reused_result_panel_replaces_long_short_empty_content_without_duplicate_controls(int shortCount)
+    {
+        var root = new GameObject("ReusedResultCanvas", typeof(RectTransform), typeof(Canvas));
+        try
+        {
+            var hudObject = new GameObject("HUD", typeof(RectTransform), typeof(HUDUI));
+            hudObject.transform.SetParent(root.transform, false);
+            var panel = new GameObject("GameOverPanel", typeof(RectTransform), typeof(Image));
+            panel.transform.SetParent(hudObject.transform, false);
+            panel.GetComponent<RectTransform>().sizeDelta = new Vector2(800f, 600f);
+            var textObject = new GameObject("GameOverText", typeof(RectTransform), typeof(TextMeshProUGUI));
+            textObject.transform.SetParent(panel.transform, false);
+            var hud = hudObject.GetComponent<HUDUI>();
+            hud.gameOverPanel = panel;
+            hud.gameOverText = textObject.GetComponent<TMP_Text>();
+            string large = "=== 比赛结果 ===\n\n";
+            for (int i = 1; i <= 12; i++) large += i + ". old-driver-" + i + " [完赛]\n";
+            large += "\nRP 与 XP 奖励说明\n";
+            for (int i = 1; i <= 12; i++) large += "old-driver-" + i + ": +100 RP, +10 XP\n";
+            hud.ShowGameOver(large);
+            ScrollRect scroll = hud.gameOverScrollRect;
+            RectTransform viewport = scroll.viewport;
+            Scrollbar scrollbar = scroll.verticalScrollbar;
+            TMP_Text title = hud.gameOverTitle;
+            int objectCount = panel.GetComponentsInChildren<Transform>(true).Length;
+            float largeHeight = scroll.content.rect.height;
+            Assert.That(largeHeight, Is.GreaterThan(viewport.rect.height),
+                "The fixture must actually overflow before testing scroll reset and shrinking.");
+            string small = "=== 比赛结果 ===\n\n";
+            for (int i = 1; i <= shortCount; i++) small += i + ". new-driver-" + i + " [完赛]\n";
+            foreach (string raw in new[] { small, string.Empty, large })
+            {
+                scroll.verticalNormalizedPosition = 0f;
+                panel.SetActive(false);
+                hud.ShowGameOver(raw);
+                Assert.That(hud.gameOverText.text, Is.EqualTo(RaceResultPresentationRules.FormatForRichText(raw)));
+                Assert.That(hud.gameOverScrollRect, Is.SameAs(scroll));
+                Assert.That(scroll.viewport, Is.SameAs(viewport));
+                Assert.That(scroll.verticalScrollbar, Is.SameAs(scrollbar));
+                Assert.That(hud.gameOverTitle, Is.SameAs(title));
+                Assert.That(scroll.content, Is.SameAs(hud.gameOverText.rectTransform));
+                Assert.That(panel.GetComponentsInChildren<Transform>(true).Length, Is.EqualTo(objectCount));
+                Assert.That(scroll.content.rect.height, Is.GreaterThanOrEqualTo(viewport.rect.height));
+                // A fitting list has no scroll range; its normalized value can be zero.
+                // Only the overflowing twelve-driver list has a meaningful top ratio.
+                if (scroll.content.rect.height > viewport.rect.height + 0.01f)
+                    Assert.That(scroll.verticalNormalizedPosition, Is.EqualTo(1f).Within(0.001f));
+                Assert.That(panel.activeSelf, Is.True);
+                if (raw != large)
+                {
+                    Assert.That(hud.gameOverText.text, Does.Not.Contain("old-driver"));
+                    Assert.That(scroll.content.rect.height, Is.LessThan(largeHeight));
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        finally { Object.DestroyImmediate(root); }
+    }

+    [TestCase(false, HeatPaymentDestination.Hand)]
+    [TestCase(true, HeatPaymentDestination.Hand)]
+    [TestCase(false, HeatPaymentDestination.Discard)]
+    [TestCase(true, HeatPaymentDestination.Discard)]
+    public void RealPaymentAdapterUsesFishOnceThenSpinsOnNextInsufficiency(
+        bool ai, HeatPaymentDestination destination)
+    {
+        var player = Player(TeamId.UK, ai);
+        player.techState = new TechTreeState(TeamId.UK)
+        { dimSumPlayedTrick = true, dimSumPlayedSpeed = true };
+        player.techState.activeNodeIds.Add("uk-l1-fish-and-chips");

+        {
+            CollectionAssert.DoesNotContain(player.deck.Hand, player.heatPaidCardsThisTurn[0]);
+            CollectionAssert.Contains(player.deck.Hand, originalHandHeat);
+            CollectionAssert.AreEqual(originalDiscard, player.deck.DiscardPile);
+        }
+        else
+        {
+            CollectionAssert.DoesNotContain(player.deck.Hand, originalHandHeat);
+            originalDiscard.Add(player.heatPaidCardsThisTurn[0]);
+            CollectionAssert.AreEqual(originalDiscard, player.deck.DiscardPile);
+        }
+        Assert.AreEqual(1, player.techState.grillSpezialHeatPaidThisTurn);
+        Assert.IsFalse(player.techState.dimSumPaidHeat);
+
+        Assert.IsFalse(manager.TryPayHeat(player, 2, 9, "engine failure", destination));
+        Assert.AreEqual(1, player.spinCounter);
+        Assert.AreEqual(9, player.position);
+        Assert.AreEqual(2, player.gear);
+        Assert.IsTrue(player.skipNextTurn);
+        Assert.AreEqual(6, player.deck.heatPool.remaining);
+        Assert.AreEqual(0, player.deck.CountPermanentHeatOutsideEngine());
+        Assert.AreEqual(2, player.heatPaidCardsThisTurn.Count);
+        Assert.AreEqual(2, player.techState.grillSpezialHeatPaidThisTurn);
+        Assert.IsFalse(player.techState.dimSumPaidHeat);
+    }

+    [TestCase(false, 0)] [TestCase(true, 0)]
+    [TestCase(false, 1)] [TestCase(true, 1)]
+    [TestCase(false, 2)] [TestCase(true, 2)]
+    [TestCase(false, 3)] [TestCase(true, 3)]
+    public void RealYangCoolingUsesOnlyHandInOrderAndNeverReclaimsDiscardReceipts(bool ai, int handMode)
+    {
+        var player = new PlayerState("Yang cooling", ai, 8, ChinaGearShiftRules.RecoverGear)
+        { teamId = TeamId.CN, techState = new TechTreeState(TeamId.CN) };
+        var speed = new CardData(CardType.Speed, 3);
+        var drawSpeed = new CardData(CardType.Speed, 2);
+        player.deck.InitializeExactOrder(new[] { speed, drawSpeed }, new HeatPool(6));
+        player.deck.DrawToHand(1);
+        player.deck.DrawHeatFromPool(1, HeatPaymentDestination.Discard, player.heatPaidCardsThisTurn);
+        CardData discardHeat = player.deck.DiscardPile[0];
+        var temporary = CardData.CreateTempHeat();
+        if (handMode == 2) player.deck.AddCardsToHand(new[] { temporary });
+        CardData permanent = null;
+        if (handMode > 0)
+        {
+            player.deck.DrawHeatFromPoolToHand(1);
+            permanent = player.deck.Hand[player.deck.Hand.Count - 1];
+        }
+        if (handMode == 3) player.deck.AddCardsToHand(new[] { temporary });
+        var method = typeof(MVPGameManager).GetMethod("ApplyYinYang", PrivateInstance);
+        Assert.IsNotNull(method);
+        int before = player.deck.heatPool.remaining;
+
+        method.Invoke(manager, new object[] { player, YinYangResult.Yang });
+
+        // Preserve the existing reverse scan within the hand-only source.
+        Assert.That(player.deck.heatPool.remaining, Is.EqualTo(before + (handMode == 1 || handMode == 2 ? 1 : 0)));
+        if (handMode > 0)
+            Assert.That(player.deck.ContainsInHand(permanent), Is.EqualTo(handMode == 3));
+        Assert.That(player.deck.ContainsInHand(temporary), Is.EqualTo(handMode == 2));
+        Assert.That(player.deck.Hand[0], Is.SameAs(speed));
+        Assert.That(player.deck.DrawPile, Is.EqualTo(new[] { drawSpeed }));
+        Assert.That(player.deck.DiscardPile, Is.EqualTo(new[] { discardHeat }));
+        Assert.That(player.heatPaidCardsThisTurn, Is.EqualTo(new[] { discardHeat }));
+
+        // A second trigger consumes only the remaining hand heat; a third cannot
+        // substitute the still-owned discard receipt or return a removed card twice.
+        method.Invoke(manager, new object[] { player, YinYangResult.Yang });
+        method.Invoke(manager, new object[] { player, YinYangResult.Yang });
+        Assert.That(player.deck.heatPool.remaining, Is.EqualTo(5));
+        Assert.That(player.deck.Hand, Is.EqualTo(new[] { speed }));
+        Assert.That(player.deck.DrawPile, Is.EqualTo(new[] { drawSpeed }));
+        Assert.That(player.deck.DiscardPile, Is.EqualTo(new[] { discardHeat }));
+        Assert.That(player.deck.CountRecordedHeat(player.heatPaidCardsThisTurn), Is.EqualTo(1));
+        Assert.That(player.deck.CountTemporaryHeatOutsideEngine(), Is.Zero);
+        Assert.That(player.deck.heatPool.remaining + player.deck.CountPermanentHeatOutsideEngine(), Is.EqualTo(6));
+        Assert.That(player.position, Is.EqualTo(8));
+        Assert.That(player.lap, Is.Zero);
+        Assert.That(player.gear, Is.EqualTo(ChinaGearShiftRules.RecoverGear));
+        Assert.That(player.spinCounter, Is.Zero);
+    }

+            Assert.AreEqual(sufficient ? 0 : available, player.deck.heatPool.remaining);
+            Assert.AreEqual(sufficient ? 2 : 0, player.deck.CountPermanentHeatOutsideEngine());
+            if (sufficient)
+            {
+                var target = destination == HeatPaymentDestination.Hand ? player.deck.Hand : player.deck.DiscardPile;
+                CollectionAssert.AreEqual(player.heatPaidCardsThisTurn, target);
+            }
+            else
+            {
+                Assert.IsEmpty(player.deck.Hand);
+                Assert.IsEmpty(player.deck.DiscardPile);
+            }
+        }
+        finally
+        {
+            manager.config = null;
+            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(config);
+        }
+    }
 }

 ### 音频技术规格
 
+2026-10-12 修订六队音频包：18 个引擎循环层 + 12 个升降挡提示，48 kHz/16-bit/mono PCM，
+约 2.29 MB。以 muted.io / Sebastien 的 CC0 原始性能车录音及 Joseph Sardin 的 CC0
+汽车天线电机实录为主体，机械换挡触感沿用项目已有 Sonniss 许可录音，进行稳定段筛选、
+轻度 EQ、统一响度和循环接缝处理；纯合成引擎包已由实录方案替换。
+生成脚本 `tools/Audio/build_team_vehicle_audio.py` 与
+`Assets/Resources/Audio/team_vehicle_profiles.json` 记录声纹、格式、峰值、RMS、SHA256 和
+可重复生成方式；原录音未认证具体汽车品牌，CN 是电机音色设计而非赛车实录。来源详情见
+`docs/audio/team-vehicle-recordings-provenance.md`；旧 GDC 2026 包来源记录继续保留。
+
 - 音乐：OGG Vorbis、44.1/48kHz、立体声、无缝循环；建议目标综合响度约 -16 LUFS。
 - 短音效：WAV、44.1/48kHz；无空间方向需求的 UI 声保持 2D，建议峰值不高于 -3 dBFS。
 - 至少分为 `Music`、`SFX`、`UI` 三个 Mixer Group，并在设置中提供独立音量和静音。
